# dunmari-frontier-108: campaign interaction extraction

17 scenes · 11 NPCs · 5 items · 39 records

Source: [transcript](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md) · segmentation revision 1

## Review notes

- Selection: 21 narrative beats, fifth-highest raw count among 34 available Dunmari beat files (110:46, 109:33, 136:32, 139:23, 108:21). Chosen for sustained multi-NPC conversations rather than mostly combat or postgame discussion. All four core PCs are listed in the session note and speak in this transcript: Kenzo, Wellby, Delwath and Seeker.
- Seventeen scenes follow actual movement and rest boundaries. The 21 editorial beats are supporting evidence, not automatic interaction boundaries. Ghesh’s extended visit, Megren’s interview, the forest communion and Dimitaur’s temple conversation each stay together.
- Dimitaur’s card distinguishes rumor, remote detection, a delivered message, scrying, and the later direct meeting. Source order includes a retrospective clarification of the scry during the rest, not an additional hour or second rest.
- Name knowledge is explicit: the party guesses at the elder’s identity earlier, but his in-world introduction is u5068. Revaka is only discussed as the wizard in the mirror; she is not summoned or released in this recording.
- Zadar’s account is hearsay through Megren, not an encounter or confirmed observation of the alleged victims. Ra’ghemdros is mentioned throughout, never actually encountered. Do not use Dimitaur’s imitation of the dragon as a direct quote from her.
- The forest is one explicitly established speaking consciousness, retained as a distinctive entity. The selected dead quartermaster is one observed individual; exclude generic dragonet groups, the unintelligent messenger spirit, and crowds of dead souls.
- Exclude incidental deity iconography, loot-provenance names, the DM’s background-history reminders about Omnis Pura and Orestes, and current-campaign PCs including absent Riswynn. Rai and Dimitaur were PCs/companions in another campaign but are NPC subjects here.
- The session metadata ends May 19, but this transcript ends with plans for a long rest. Played interactions are dated May 18; no unseen morning encounter is invented.
- Version 3 adds important-item cards and one dominant type per scene; existing scene boundaries, indexes, dates and NPC encounter ranges are preserved. Extra NPC quotes stay inside the same conversations.
- The jade card is a collection, not a claim that the party has an intact hand: four fragments are already held and one is still sought. The staff is a separate object. The mirror is only discussed and the phylactery’s form is not established. Later canonical item histories and powers are not imported into the session.
- Major NPC quotes now include three from Ghesh, two from Megren and four from Dimitaur, without adding NPC interaction records. The short source excerpt on the circlet is explicitly Delwath’s inventory description, not speech by the object or an NPC.
- Scene types use dominant activity: the guided journey is travel, the memorial investigation and magical communion are exploration, and the temple exchange is conversation. Existing source boundaries are unchanged.

## Ghesh · NPC

Kenzo gambles with Ghesh, buys his discretion and guidance, races him on the way to Megren, and pays him after the introduction.

Identity: [Ghesh](../../../../People/Other%20Nonhumans/Ghesh.md)

> I'm always happy to play dice with people who seemingly don't know the rules.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u1180–u1180](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L1180) — DM voices this NPC during the surrounding direct exchange.

> Well, now it's a bargain. A few little coins isn't enough to buy my silence.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u1539–u1540](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L1539) — Ghesh demands a meaningful payment before promising secrecy.

> I, I would not mourn at her funeral.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u2210–u2210](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L2210) — Ghesh answers Kenzo’s questions about whether he would miss Ra’ghemdros.

### Scenes 4, 5, 6 · interacted · star

Kenzo gambles with Ghesh, bargains for secrecy and an introduction, travels with him to Megren, and pays the agreed reward.

Continuity: One continuous visit with Ghesh: the dice bargain becomes a guided journey and introduction, ending with payment. Topic changes, party coordination, the race and Megren’s intervening dialogue remain within the encounter.

Complete interaction: [u0849–u2688](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L849)

## Megren · NPC

Megren tells Kenzo she traded the jade fragment to Ra’ghemdros, describes the dragon’s dangerous lair, and passes on rumors of an elder despite fearing reprisals.

Identity: [Megren](../../../../People/Other%20Nonhumans/Megren.md)

> But if you want to take a risk, you can take risks. I don't want to be turned into a pool of acid.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u2605–u2606](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L2605) — DM voices this NPC during the surrounding direct exchange.

> Like, whoever found it didn't even know what they had found. Why would you just leave that sitting out and not protect it or anything?

Pull quote (dialogue): [u2342–u2343](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L2342) — Megren explains her opportunistic view of finding the jade on the mainland.

## Ra'ghemdros · NPC

The party learns that Ra’ghemdros holds the jade, rules the dragonets through fear and rewards, and is Cha’mutte’s daughter, then debates negotiation, theft, or fighting her.

Identity: [Ra'ghemdros](../../../../People/Other%20Nonhumans/Ra%27ghemdros.md)

### Scenes 4, 5 · mentioned · star

Ghesh describes Ra’ghemdros’s control over the dragonets and the rewards and dangers of serving her.

Continuity: One encounter continues across these location/time boundaries; other speakers and brief asides remain inside its source range.

## Dimitaur · NPC

After hearing rumors, locating and scrying the elder, the party bypasses Dimitaur’s trap and earns a conversation about Rai, Ra’ghemdros, Drankor, and Apollyon.

Identity: [Dimitaur](../../../../People/PCs/Great%20War/Companions/Dimitaur.md)

> The important thing is to just not keep the plans you make.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u5103–u5103](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L5103) — DM voices this NPC during the surrounding direct exchange.

> The gods can only hear.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u4842–u4842](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L4842) — Dimitaur, still posing as the temple’s divine voice, admits he cannot see Kenzo’s dramatic display.

> I only stayed there a few months waiting for him, and it got dark and scary, and there was this, like, aura of fear that radiated out through the whole place coming from Apollyon's tomb. And every morning, I woke up wanting to run away, and eventually I did.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u5347–u5348](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L5347) — Dimitaur recounts why he abandoned Drankor while waiting for Rai.

> I'd pick an easier quest.

Pull quote (dialogue): [u5448–u5448](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L5448) — Dimitaur answers Kenzo’s request for advice on recovering the jade from the dragon.

### Scenes 6 · mentioned · supporting

### Scenes 13 · observed · supporting

Delwath scries the elderly dragonet preparing a stone trap inside the Temple of the Sibyl and ignoring the messenger.

Known as: elder dragonet; possible Dimitaur

Continuity: The source explains this scry after describing the completed rest, then explicitly places it earlier during the same rest. This is a separate remote observation, not a new conversational exchange.

### Scenes 17 · interacted · star

After bypassing his trap, the party earns Dimitaur’s trust and questions him about Rai, the jade, the dragon, Drankor, and Apollyon.

Known as: elder dragonet; names himself Dimitaur at u5068

Continuity: One temple conversation continues through jokes, thought-reading, gifts, tactical asides and many subject changes. It ends when the party proposes further discussion and a long rest.

Introduction (description, [u4882–u4883](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L4882)):

> Um, and he's walking slowly. He's carrying like a short staff in one hand and a wand in the other that he's still kind of pointing at you.

## Rai · NPC

The party uses its connection to Rai to seek the elder’s help and learns from Dimitaur about Rai’s disappearance and failed attempt to heal the land.

Identity: [Rai](../../../../People/PCs/Great%20War/Rai.md)

### Scenes 9 · mentioned · supporting

The party recalls Kenzo’s dream of Rai and an elderly dragonet while deciding how to locate the rumored elder.

Complete interaction: [u3171–u3401](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L3171)

## Rai’s jade hand fragments · ITEM

The party learns Megren gave the sought fifth fragment to Ra’ghemdros, weighs how to recover it, and shows Dimitaur the four fragments already held in Kenzo’s staff.

Identity: [Jade Piece of Rai's Hand](../../../../Campaigns/Dunmari%20Frontier%20Campaign/Treasure/Jade%20Piece%20of%20Rai%27s%20Hand.md)

> big green gem that was just sitting there. Like, whoever found it didn't even know what they had found. Why would you just leave that sitting out and not protect it or anything?

## Staff of the Forest Soul · ITEM

Kenzo uses his staff while communing with the memorial forest, seeks an image to preserve in it, and reveals its concealed jade fragments to Dimitaur.

Identity: [Staff of the Forest Soul](../../../../Campaigns/Dunmari%20Frontier%20Campaign/Treasure/Staff%20of%20the%20Forest%20Soul.md)

### Scenes 6 · mentioned · supporting

## Gold circlet with four alexandrite gems · ITEM

Kenzo offers the circlet for Ghesh’s secrecy and guidance, then pays it over after the introduction to Megren.

> the golden circlet set with four green alexandrite gems from Grimbaskal's hoard, worth at least 500 gold.

Source quote (dialogue): [u1614–u1614](../../../dunmar-frontier/dunmari-frontier-108/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-108-source-cleaned.md?plain=1#L1614) — Delwath identifies the particular treasure proposed as payment; this is not an NPC line.

## Apollyon’s phylactery · ITEM

The party hopes completing Rai’s jade hand will reveal the phylactery’s location and questions Dimitaur about finding and destroying it.
